# Triumph TF: aftermarket parts research catalog

Research candidates gathered from public web sources (manufacturer pages, dealers, retailers, magazine tests, testers, forums). Each product keeps its source and a confidence: high = the manufacturer lists the part number and fitment; medium = a dealer or major retailer does; unverified = inferred or title-only. Nothing here is verified evidence, nothing is shown to riders yet, and no fitment, price or performance claim should be repeated without checking the source. Performance claims are the maker's or tester's, with their conditions.

Files: cg-triumph.json, chassis.json, dealer-triumph-genuine.json, engine.json, ms-triumph.json, suspension.json, triumph-aftermarket.json, triumph-feedback.json, tuning.json. Built by scripts/aftermarket/build_catalog.py.

Counts: 4242 products, 17043 part numbers, 31 rider/tester findings, 5 project-bike builds, 9 of 9 in-scope bike-years with at least one mapped product.

## Project-bike builds

### [Triumph TF 450-RC Edition (factory limited edition, Ricky Carmichael spec)](https://www.cyclenews.com/2025/02/article/2025-triumph-tf-450-rc-edition-review/) — Triumph TF 450-RC Edition 2025, Cycle News (also Dirt Bike Magazine 2025-12-14, 1000PS 2024-11-18) 2025-02-07

- handlebars: ODI RC4 / Podium Flight RC Bend bar — Carmichael-preferred narrower bend; found: More comfortable, universal attack position than TF 250-X ProTaper
- seat: unspecified Gripper seat cover — Grip under acceleration; found: Keeps rider in place on hard throttle
- engine-cover: Hinson Clutch cover — Clutch upgrade/protection; found: 'Nice touch'; not evaluated
- holeshot-device: XTrig Holeshot device — Starts; found: Not evaluated
- tires: Dunlop Geomax MX34 80/100-21, 110/90-19 — Traction; found: No specific observation
- brake-protection: unspecified Front disc guard — Disc protection; found: Not evaluated
- electronics: Triumph Wi-Fi module / MX Tune Pro with RC map — Map selection; found: RC map added bottom-end; 'more hit, more pull, more overrev'; presets not customizable

### [Keefer Inc TF 250-X FMF exhaust setup](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/?p=28483) — Triumph TF 250-X 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2024-12-09

- exhaust: FMF 4.1 RCT Ti muffler — Mid-range and corner pull; found: Stronger 2nd/3rd gear corner pull, longer mid-range, slightly softer off-idle crack, quieter/deeper; ~1 lb lighter system
- exhaust: FMF Megabomb headpipe — Paired with 4.1; found: Needed adjustment to line up with engine mount holes
- sprockets-gearing: unspecified 49T rear sprocket (13/49) — Shorter gearing; found: Keefer's running setup
- electronics-ecu: Triumph 2025 ECU spec (dealer update) — Improved mapping; found: Pulling power better with FMF

### [Dirt Bike Test (UK) TF 250-X test bike changes](https://dirtbiketest.com/bike-tests/2024-triumph-tf-250-x/) — Triumph TF 250-X 2024, Dirt Bike Test (UK) 2024-10

- tires: Dunlop Geomax MX34, 110 rear — Replace Pirelli MX32 100 rear; found: Chassis felt about the same
- sprockets-gearing: Pro X 49T rear sprocket — Tighter spread, more third gear mid-corner; found: Chain +2 links, axle moved back
- chain: unspecified Chain lengthened two links — Accommodate 49T and 110 rear

### [MotoOnline race-spec TF 250-X demo bike](https://motoonline.com.au/?p=188223) — Triumph TF 250-X 2024, MotoOnline (Australia) 

- exhaust: Akrapovic Full exhaust system — Power; found: With race map, could use third gear out of corners
- electronics-ecu: GET Ignition with Wi-Fi tuner attachment — Race map; found: Combined with exhaust per above
- holeshot-device: XTrig Holeshot device — Starts; found: Not evaluated
- suspension-springs: Triumph Stock springs (race bike) vs optional medium/hard on test bike — Rider weight; found: Stock too soft for 90 kg reviewer; hard springs best

### [Bike Review AU accessory-fitted TF 250-X](https://bikereview.com.au/?p=137533) — Triumph TF 250-X 2024, Bike Review (Australia) 2024-07-22

- exhaust: Akrapovic Full titanium exhaust — Power/weight; found: Louder, more low-mid torque; listed 0.47 kg lighter
- suspension-springs: Triumph Medium and hard rear springs — Heavier riders (stock rated 80 kg); found: More confidence on jumps and corners for 98 kg rider
- seat: Triumph Performance gripper seat and seat cover — Grip; found: More planted cornering
- holeshot-device: XTrig Holeshot device — Starts; found: Not commented
- electronics: Athena LC-GPA launch control module (front fender) — Launch display; found: Not tested

- (triumph-aftermarket.json) Stock spec (for KB context): KYB 48mm AOS coil fork + KYB shock, Brembo calipers, Galfer discs 260/220mm (Galfer is OEM supplier), DirtStar 7000 rims, ProTaper ACF bars, ODI grips, 7075 clamps (MXA 2025 TF250-X first look).
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) Pro Circuit part numbers come from Cycle Gear 'Manufacturer Product #' fields; procircuit.com not fetched.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) TF 450-X / 450-E 2026 fitment rarely listed by aftermarket sellers yet; most 450 parts list only 2025 RC Edition / 2026 C.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) No suspension tuner revalve service (Enzo, Factory Connection, Pro Circuit, Race Tech, Technical Touch) with Triumph-specific listing found; only FC fork springs.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) Akrapovic aftermarket (non-Triumph) Evolution part numbers for TF not found; Partseurope pages need JavaScript.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) No Wiseco, Vertex or JE piston for TF found in searches; only Pro-X.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) Rekluse blog part numbers read from image filenames - verify.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) MXA 2024 test: stock shock bottoming, low-end bog (mapping), notchy shifting, poor rear traction on 1.85 rim - tuning targets.
- (triumph-aftermarket.json) Rekluse blog mentions a Triumph recall for a new clutch centre hub and plates (years not stated).
- (triumph-feedback.json) No dyno-measured results for any aftermarket exhaust (FMF, Pro Circuit, Akrapovic, Yoshimura RS-12 listing seen at a retailer but no test).
- (triumph-feedback.json) No part numbers reported by any source for TF aftermarket parts.
- (triumph-feedback.json) No independent tests found for the 2026 TF 250-X update or TF 250-C.
- (triumph-feedback.json) No Triumph factory team (SMX/MXGP) bike-build feature with parts list found within budget.
